##### What was that? Meta and Netflix upgrade ad capabilities, AI copyright doesn’t exist yet, the US experiences unexpected job losses, Musk’s X reinvents creator subscriptions, and the rest is…

This newsletter is sponsored by you… and you need to read the [newsletter that came out last night](https://substack.com/redirect/1a81d8c2-a2b9-481e-9a8a-a8d779d83a80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), titled: **AI won’t kill marketing. We’ll do it ourselves.**

> Before you go further, Attention is like money – you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VaNH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/36d4c86a-11fa-44e8-b34c-41ff1b606bc2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Supreme Court declines [AI copyright case](https://substack.com/redirect/5710cb88-69ba-4f8c-a449-cdc60257dccc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), extending legal setback for [AI-generated material](https://substack.com/redirect/1fafdd10-088b-4385-9324-18db31ac9088?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* The U.S. [unexpectedly loses](https://substack.com/redirect/dad65548-7040-4423-9991-491d6feef7ec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) 92,000 jobs in [February](https://substack.com/redirect/d7bb3958-6eed-414e-9791-0def0de2512f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g); unemployment rate rises to 4.4%.
* X reinvents [Creator Subscriptions](https://substack.com/redirect/d28c5a51-6a05-4def-95a1-dcde57074d20?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with new features, like [exclusive threads](https://substack.com/redirect/3cd3fb0b-b070-4ca7-9500-01262d4cb8f0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), and shareable cards.
* [OpenAI](https://substack.com/redirect/23b0f20e-e8a9-4cee-b305-f30124660fd2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) held early talks with [The Trade Desk](https://substack.com/redirect/54b2dc73-6e69-4bc1-bfb7-553d22eb7de5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to sell ads.
	+ The Trade Desk CEO [Jeff Green](https://substack.com/redirect/f1f5dfd8-baa1-472e-997c-4f24ee4e8894?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) dropped $148M on [company stock](https://substack.com/redirect/5b731527-0d55-4f1d-8c35-e63ca929a1ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Meta](https://substack.com/redirect/400ce259-cdf6-4fe5-aba0-da450e941deb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has a new way to measure social engagement ([because clicks don’t cut it](https://substack.com/redirect/99d03c24-8aaa-494e-9d11-3906b584c356?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)).
* Google to introduce [30-second unskippable ads](https://substack.com/redirect/148eb0c3-cdb7-48c6-beaa-3518f7c2bdd4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on YouTube TV [worldwide](https://substack.com/redirect/02e781bf-317f-4b9c-8e4a-05b5e548c7c4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!zM4F!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/b4ad7413-63b0-4d14-b8fa-93d622e1ca5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Meta sends private AI glasses footage to Kenya with [few safeguards](https://substack.com/redirect/43648f85-177d-468b-baf1-a0cb610fe4f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) - and Europe’s privacy regulators [have come](https://substack.com/redirect/6f1772b4-207d-44f4-b2e0-acb1d152bdab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) knocking and [a lawsuit](https://substack.com/redirect/42f99403-e6af-4901-a882-8dc83db934f2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the United States.
	+ [Report](https://substack.com/redirect/f9308882-c2ae-490b-822a-b6aa9a8a6784?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): She came out of the bathroom naked, employee says.
* [IAB](https://substack.com/redirect/d95bbeee-5b1e-4ecf-8f0a-984ce0aed0d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): UK’s digital ad market reaches £40.5bn. 
	+ 57% expect digital budgets to increase in 2026, with video, retail media and DOOH forecast to see the strongest gains.
* [UK social media ban](https://substack.com/redirect/932fe625-67e1-4bfc-b182-1417789f2e51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for under-16s consultation begins.
	+ [UK](https://substack.com/redirect/79bf82a7-8021-4838-b1f0-31f5b58858e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to delay [difficult decisions](https://substack.com/redirect/52519743-2173-4209-94fa-47fdc160b446?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on AI copyright rules.
* ‘[We’ve lost a giant](https://substack.com/redirect/e519280e-4f08-4544-aa1a-59aa2f3108c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: Tributes paid after [Annette King](https://substack.com/redirect/eb580931-f6c3-4077-8464-54643fa46f43?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) dies aged 57.
* [OpenAI](https://substack.com/redirect/e86838a7-222b-45f5-b617-e93344840ca6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), in desperate need of a win, launches [GPT-5.4](https://substack.com/redirect/f2658cab-7bd2-4959-99d9-121a152b52e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* CBP tapped into the [online advertising ecosystem](https://substack.com/redirect/45558b1f-657f-47b7-92a0-78b44c13c000?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to track peoples’ movements.
* [Axel Springer](https://substack.com/redirect/81119505-21a7-4808-a608-53130ae2c534?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) buys [Telegraph](https://substack.com/redirect/06c2b39a-f8c2-41ee-a49d-0f94a4b5963c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in £575mn deal, a totally unexpected move?
* [Pentagon’s supply chain risk label](https://substack.com/redirect/49b1667c-23eb-4d17-adec-2999882a7d81?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Anthropic narrower than initially implied, [company](https://substack.com/redirect/6c1f179e-f558-45c5-ad39-addc9aa81af7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) says.
* [Outrage](https://substack.com/redirect/dc48d3f2-dcfb-44c8-85cb-c4316e6f6213?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as website claiming to check women’s ‘[body count](https://substack.com/redirect/1ac2cd98-7695-4bd9-a169-a2382743e2f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ goes viral.
* Paramount+ and [HBO Max](https://substack.com/redirect/03187673-2d67-4ad2-a224-2995f723787d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to become [one streaming service](https://substack.com/redirect/b3b63cd6-2f39-49f3-9891-b0a4628488a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), Ellison says.

* Oracle to start layoffs which [may go up to 30,000](https://substack.com/redirect/7353462d-7fcb-447a-a044-b8d2e077be54?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as company faces [AI Cash Crunch](https://substack.com/redirect/717f16fd-ef03-45f7-b8cb-a3e550b45d12?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* AI actor Tilly Norwood’s world is [expanding](https://substack.com/redirect/16bfd26c-522f-444c-ba5b-ca04ec29b344?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with the ‘[Tillyverse](https://substack.com/redirect/be27316b-bbed-4657-bd1f-487d1770d84d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).’
* Cluely CEO Roy Lee admits to [publicly lying about revenue numbers](https://substack.com/redirect/e4c8aa3a-59ab-46ca-8c04-97c9ac61ac89?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) last year.
* BBC says [iPlayer](https://substack.com/redirect/dbb8c048-1332-4c51-b460-9fab77d77a41?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) could host rival broadcasters as corporation sets out charter renewal funding vision.
* [LLMs](https://substack.com/redirect/9d7179d9-4be8-447b-97c2-2cb10abaf43e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) can [unmask pseudonymous users](https://substack.com/redirect/adc5b919-6b13-45ce-81d3-69fc468b3780?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at scale with surprising accuracy.
* [The secretive company](https://substack.com/redirect/bc9e2b40-eccd-4fa2-9e56-d0755c2be21f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) filling video game sites with gambling and AI.
	+ Online ads just became the [internet’s biggest malware machine](https://substack.com/redirect/caade709-71b6-42b1-a36f-2f591488c425?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Apple [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/a6e5f959-9db4-4129-a1e8-cefe6fe5861a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) first-ever budget MacBook, it’s [beautiful](https://substack.com/redirect/0d562052-e3cf-4738-876b-d48768150418?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [I told ya!](https://substack.com/redirect/1a81d8c2-a2b9-481e-9a8a-a8d779d83a80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) OpenAI [scales back](https://substack.com/redirect/631bba97-ce89-4f33-a62e-23bbc400690f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shopping plans for [ChatGPT](https://substack.com/redirect/ded2cc5d-081a-49b3-8e0b-13391c74260c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Meta tests [AI shopping research tool](https://substack.com/redirect/3fcd719e-e195-4dc4-94af-b3a1940fbf53?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to rival ChatGPT, Gemini.
* [Google](https://substack.com/redirect/cd9c45a6-187d-48c5-bcd3-9bea6cfd42f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) faces lawsuit after Gemini chatbot allegedly instructed [man to kill himself](https://substack.com/redirect/275b4e56-d380-4685-8aa4-0801bb69fb32?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

	+ Inside an [AI Slop Factory](https://substack.com/redirect/0fe6f5b8-554f-423f-826f-0fc49587d225?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), made for ad fraud.
		- “This means each page may cost less than $2.25 to generate while creating hundreds of distinct ad serving opportunities. At that price, it doesn’t take too many impressions to recoup production costs. Everything else is pure margin.”

> 🫠 Happy International Women’s Day? Almost a third of generation Z men and boys think a wife should [obey her husband](https://substack.com/redirect/894d0ec1-8d7f-45eb-a25d-aec369fbb35c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), according to a global survey of 23,000 people that found young men hold more traditional views about gender roles than older generations.

> A third (33%) of Gen Z males also said a husband should have the final word on important decisions, according to the [29-country survey](https://substack.com/redirect/80cb86af-43a4-4451-86c9-8a707067c0d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), which included Great Britain, the US, Brazil, Australia and India.

> 🫒 Report Break: A recent RTS study of 1,000 shoppers found that, on average, Gen Z consumers (born between 1997 and 2012) made 20 purchases on [Roblox](https://substack.com/redirect/7b47faab-e1be-41a2-86ce-9277c4a31835?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over the last 12 months. This represents a 54% year-on-year rise, outpacing TikTok’s 10%. Gen Z placed the [highest number of Roblox orders](https://substack.com/redirect/798354c6-9790-432d-b36e-4f8a80d5eeca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), making 2.4× more purchases than any other age group.

> 🦀 Report Break: Most marketers, 52%, take a mixed approach when it comes to managing influencer marketing, working with both in-house and external partners. Thirty-two percent of respondents primarily use external partners while 16% primarily handle relationships in-house, though the latter group appears to be growing, per ANA.

> Just 51% of marketers have [full clarity into influencer payments](https://substack.com/redirect/0fbc3694-6579-4351-8186-7dc1b2aaa319?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). On the agency side, 39% said the agency they use does not employ nontransparent compensation methods, while 31% said agencies did use such methods.

	+ [WTF](https://substack.com/redirect/7838dfd1-a396-4966-837a-8a28d7598396?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is Meta’s Manus tool?
* Meta to introduce [monthly invoicing system](https://substack.com/redirect/44560f3a-9c83-46b4-a53b-bec86f803b3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for select advertisers.
* ‘[Not a big part of the work](https://substack.com/redirect/33b582d4-c726-4d3d-bdb8-6476aa1bd0f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: Meta’s LLM bet has yet to touch its core ads business.
* News Corp is essentially an AI ‘[input company](https://substack.com/redirect/05dea99f-47e1-4395-92bc-e4f5d98a041b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’, chief executive says, after US$150m deal with Meta.
	+ News Corp CEO Robert Thomson warns AI companies scraping without paying: ‘[We’re coming for you](https://substack.com/redirect/1d533ddd-3ab2-4d75-bafd-6639f69c61bd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).’
* Facebook and some [Meta business tools went down](https://substack.com/redirect/057b7dc0-c1e7-4afc-906b-36a8a72e5098?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) without reason?

	+ Meta Platforms asks EU court to annul ‘urgent’ decision to [ban behavioral ads](https://substack.com/redirect/108437ba-df2c-429c-98e8-45680ee14822?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Mark Zuckerberg downplays [Meta’s own research](https://substack.com/redirect/58efbf4a-9e3d-47bf-96e5-43b068f3a239?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in New Mexico child safety trial.
	+ "I agree that the graph says that, I am not familiar with what methodology we were using to estimate this," Zuckerberg said.
* Meta ‘[destroyed](https://substack.com/redirect/e95c82df-d3d2-4b5e-a6ae-8ddec4be3973?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ key evidence in Twiggy Forrest’s scam ad fight.
* ICYMI: Meta hit with a class action lawsuit over [smart glasses’ privacy claims](https://substack.com/redirect/e6889e56-f193-45d9-b474-633d69880b64?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

* Americans stranded in the Mideast used [WhatsApp and social media](https://substack.com/redirect/1d4db5d1-33fb-4881-806c-2808be7daf56?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to figure out how to get home.
	+ Meta recommends [Mullvad and AmneziaVPN](https://substack.com/redirect/4f436540-42e6-4f2f-8a96-6c1eb9c91849?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for WhatsApp users encountering network disruptions.
* [WhatsApp](https://substack.com/redirect/e43ea73a-11b0-483c-8076-fa4989d06a9a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) paid subscription [reportedly in development](https://substack.com/redirect/ae1ad579-b2e5-4b6d-8e35-d4e3024ed1e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with additional customisation options, up to 20 pinned chats.
* [Meta’s Oversight Board](https://substack.com/redirect/938afd31-5fbd-40db-a282-c939abda3ca8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) races to govern the AI surge.

> 🫩 Report Break: In nearly all countries surveyed, more people say that others in their country have somewhat or very good morals than say their compatriots display somewhat or very bad levels of morality. The United States is the only place we surveyed where more adults (ages 18 and older) describe the [morality and ethics of others living in the country as bad](https://substack.com/redirect/5b59d9a2-2ee1-4001-a939-5d030698b5be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) (53%) than as good (47%).

> Only one in four Americans approves of ​U.S. strikes on [Iran](https://substack.com/redirect/b53dc24c-59ab-475d-8ced-0f0549b2fac1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that have plunged the Middle East into chaos, while about half — including one in four Republicans — believe President Donald Trump ‌is too willing to use military force, according to a [Reuters/Ipsos poll](https://substack.com/redirect/fdcec3eb-2f99-4003-bec2-91848cd6d05a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!xdrk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F504f7135-86f7-4edd-a923-b08f71d3eeb3_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/416f6e37-a910-4681-804b-9ba8f02300d7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* YouTube direct messaging is going live in [more regions](https://substack.com/redirect/3f50963f-4ef3-4a0c-9c96-6dba32aeb9fe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Pareto principle appears to apply to YouTube viewing, says [Thinkbox](https://substack.com/redirect/212f314b-52bb-45c0-9b0d-cb4c54ffd5de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ According to Barb data, the heaviest 25% of YouTube viewers account for around 90% of viewing hours on YouTube while the other three-quarters of users account for just 10% of YouTube consumption by time viewed.
* YouTube now offers [an auto-generated, nonstop playlist](https://substack.com/redirect/54916f74-8f79-4cc6-89c0-cab551a18b65?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) tailored to your listening habits called Mix.
* Are you a podcaster [who signed a deal](https://substack.com/redirect/7509f4ce-e28b-452f-88cb-0b3fe262f0a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Netflix? Better not post those clips on YouTube!
	+ Netflix CFO says video podcasts ‘[overindex on mobile](https://substack.com/redirect/c5c40899-2a3c-488a-a7e3-9245fa51b3fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ compared to scripted programming.
* YouTube publishes [football edition of Culture & Trends](https://substack.com/redirect/5568f5f4-32ea-45cb-929a-c0b09e820c79?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) report.
* YouTube Americas Leader Tara Walpert Levy says measurement proves [creators do TV ads](https://substack.com/redirect/d2f3fae5-9d54-437e-8a77-f49551e941ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) best.
	+ “[Creator TV](https://substack.com/redirect/1f8f515e-cb6d-4dd3-82df-9617aa91c3b2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” is a thing, and Spotter wants to sell it to big brands.
* A persistent YouTube bug is [placing immovable ads](https://substack.com/redirect/9d98dde1-f9fc-4e21-bf96-7d1fbc759c3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in fullscreen videos.
* The [future of horror movies](https://substack.com/redirect/e0c18ac1-960e-4ead-a804-0fca38a1d5c4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is on YouTube.
	+ YouTube exec says streamer could [one day put sports events](https://substack.com/redirect/4402d5ac-8d48-465c-a17c-81833fe4c593?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ‘behind a subscription.’
* YouTube announces expansion of [Studio payment activity capabilities](https://substack.com/redirect/264834cd-dd7a-437a-a958-e227a3d590a6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for creators with multiple channels.

> 🛼 Report Break: Creator integrations generate [nearly five times more](https://substack.com/redirect/e784dbe4-3373-490e-94fb-65b4877754bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) minutes of viewer attention than CTV ads, for the same level of spend. The research also shows that Brands can achieve a 37% increase in total attention reach by reallocating just 10% of their CTV budget to YouTube Creator partnerships, with zero additional investment required.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!SRmL!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6ddeabac-2fb9-4449-8c6d-6f3b735dce87_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/4b913d7b-45ed-49c3-9970-5befca35ee21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Google Patent](https://substack.com/redirect/95b50ef3-a76e-4496-beec-9046ec47ae3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Sending online searchers to [AI-generated landing pages](https://substack.com/redirect/01511258-827f-4919-8691-be4830fe1d59?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over your site.
* Google updates [AI recipe results](https://substack.com/redirect/bbdb5efc-6da8-498c-8604-1a8b460f20d3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to send more traffic.
* Google contacts advertisers with [a mandatory EU political ads deadline](https://substack.com/redirect/c351e360-ce44-41f3-af99-2d2c179fb0df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Google’s Liz Reid says LLMs [unlock](https://substack.com/redirect/5289e83b-2a7e-4c79-bf84-f2c12d519dbb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) audio and video indexing.
* Google Search rolls out [Gemini’s Canvas](https://substack.com/redirect/69c43af4-c8bb-4fa5-8b21-355776e7bd09?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in AI Mode to all US users.
* [Tim Sweeney](https://substack.com/redirect/f653c9ab-7fde-4a4c-af54-b89c1ee887d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) signed away his right to criticize Google’s app store until 2032.
* Google Merchant Center adds “[build to order”](https://substack.com/redirect/39808ab2-f6bc-4983-b399-c72fc0eb9f14?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for vehicle listings.
* Google gives [CEO Sundar Pichai new pay deal](https://substack.com/redirect/e7137771-0889-43c3-a282-687b8e47c0e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) worth up to $692mn.
* Google to [disable Customer Match uploads](https://substack.com/redirect/0d33feee-d35a-43f8-96b3-3926c18e2529?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Ads API.

> [IAB Europe State of Readiness Sustainability:](https://substack.com/redirect/171b623b-dbe3-438b-8555-caa208a00bb4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Nearly 30% of respondents now list ad fraud as a major ecosystem challenge, compared with single-digit levels in earlier surveys. Meanwhile, a significant share of respondents (32%) have not and do not plan to publish sustainability reports, illustrating uneven readiness across the ecosystem. 

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!kquR!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9fd6b2a4-7806-4786-8201-e116147ae5ef_2072x1156.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/df5c68b7-b0ac-4d3d-aeac-124a3771e4e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

---

* Google removes [JavaScript SEO warning](https://substack.com/redirect/d8495bbd-bfe3-4cdc-8970-368ffc934303?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), says it’s outdated.
* Google Ads API enforces [daily minimum budget](https://substack.com/redirect/4f91a47e-52c0-4298-853e-ee0621615c6b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Demand Gen campaigns.
* Google ends its [30 percent app store fee](https://substack.com/redirect/83871b4e-4b98-4617-a145-c0292a2b8463?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and welcomes third-party app stores.
* Google’s new command-line tool can [plug OpenClaw](https://substack.com/redirect/750e4310-435a-46b7-b47c-09d22e7d86c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into your Workspace data.
* NotebookLM can now summarize research in ‘[cinematic](https://substack.com/redirect/a00fe65a-5c44-4ce5-b875-c0ec807a949d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ video overviews.
* Google Ads expands [recurring billing policy](https://substack.com/redirect/62c3b445-1971-4618-ae45-671bcded1013?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Changes to [Google Discover](https://substack.com/redirect/e4844e3b-2672-4061-a355-f3b4a23068cf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) drive 46% decline in traffic at [Reach Plc](https://substack.com/redirect/e7ee3376-c441-47b9-a964-09441482e3b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Google [clarifies](https://substack.com/redirect/5a2268d9-20ee-4f60-815b-264e2783f9aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) how it picks thumbnails for Search, Discover.

> 🛼 Report Break: Google’s [self-referencing in AI Mode](https://substack.com/redirect/94e1bb32-54c6-45a9-96c8-5490a466869f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has risen to more than three times its level nine months ago, according to a [new SE Ranking report](https://substack.com/redirect/9afc907d-3a39-46b9-8eaf-ca8dd18ef2a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) analyzing 1.3 million citations. Google now accounts for 17% of all AI Mode citations, more than YouTube, Facebook, Reddit, Amazon, Indeed, and Zillow combined.

> Meanwhile, [Ahrefs](https://substack.com/redirect/a83fec7a-ff3e-4971-bca0-22856f4296ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reports YouTube is the most-cited domain in AI Overviews overall and has grown 34% over the past six months.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!J7DY!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Facd06a12-7508-4bfa-91b1-8c434a20fc84_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/280e3d44-4ec2-4884-9147-7eb5660e68ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* X officially launches [a Listen feature](https://substack.com/redirect/e8e39584-a5db-4f91-a378-20e2f5c76a57?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Articles, powered by Grok Voice.
* A new pitch deck reveals X Is using [Grok to sell brand safety](https://substack.com/redirect/181fcaf2-b9be-4d3b-a0bc-8d24ed8f6e5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* X adds [paid partnership labels](https://substack.com/redirect/0fbaeb2a-2d4c-437c-9814-c2977460693a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for sponsored posts.
* X gets rid of its popular night mode [in-app setting](https://substack.com/redirect/42b211c7-2844-4dd2-abc2-6b5801d6fbb0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Musk’s X will stop paying people for sharing [unlabeled AI-generated war footage](https://substack.com/redirect/b2ef2a49-ed19-4bda-baac-75173d85cf65?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* X tests [post filtering](https://substack.com/redirect/dee9eeb1-f522-4be8-8543-674d3d433fe2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for user profiles on the web.
* Musk’s X usage [declined in the EU](https://substack.com/redirect/101fd568-b066-4f9a-ade7-f35cdc74c377?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the second half of 2025.
	+ [xAI](https://substack.com/redirect/8e790cba-8122-4785-b221-685881d2ad65?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) loses bid [to halt](https://substack.com/redirect/75c1f1dc-683a-4a85-bc1b-6f5b3057145e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) California AI data disclosure law.
* X [refused](https://substack.com/redirect/bfb4a8f6-299b-47c6-bc47-fd88c0039777?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) stay in Coimisiún na Meán challenge.
* X is planning to redesign the audience and [reply control options](https://substack.com/redirect/1e6b15c6-8b54-4cf3-8c10-30bb1198cc0f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on iOS.
* X taps William Shatner to give out invites to its payments service, [X Money](https://substack.com/redirect/561c2bfe-168a-4117-bb83-5d1c7cf7b829?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Elon Musk’s X begins testing standalone [X Chat App for iOS](https://substack.com/redirect/5184990a-8b96-4033-925e-643a5b490fe2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Musk tells jury ‘[people read too much](https://substack.com/redirect/77787854-53fd-417a-8dc2-41e62f07fd4b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ into his posts.
* X testing new feature to [block image editing](https://substack.com/redirect/3a71ece9-f0a3-469c-8662-7ec4d44e0dba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Grok AI.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!85wj!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F20a7b272-bbeb-4fe3-a619-d76e41822174_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/31a2d1ed-ce7d-4c86-b2f7-22b8bdc39e47?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Snapchat [releases](https://substack.com/redirect/90ad4042-de9c-4bee-b579-009bce286f82?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) new data and insights about movie marketing. 
	+ 44% of Daily Snapchatters have visited a movie theater in the past six months, which is 1.3x higher than non-Snapchatters and this trend is only increasing in the near future.
	+ Snapchat published [another report with help from GWI](https://substack.com/redirect/aeab93b5-6615-4648-bb8b-fab29260fa2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) repositioning itself as a B2B marketing platform.
* Snapchat bets on Gen Z cricket frenzy with [‘Cricket in a Snap’ ad suite](https://substack.com/redirect/fdaded76-3df4-4966-8100-2985d9fc3f79?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Pinterest](https://substack.com/redirect/fe299cfa-268a-4257-9bff-4357980b417a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and Amazon launch integration allowing creators to link Amazon storefronts.
	+ Pinterest is trying to revive its ad sales growth and has [reshuffled some of its leadership](https://substack.com/redirect/636b8acd-8f94-40d5-9bf2-693bde75046a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to help with that effort.
* Audible launches a cheaper ‘[Standard](https://substack.com/redirect/ad3c374a-6a64-45ab-9579-357abb7e8230?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ subscription plan, [challenging](https://substack.com/redirect/33428811-da69-458c-9077-f1c4daba2f03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Spotify.
* Yahoo is [selling Engadget](https://substack.com/redirect/44b47e86-a65b-4a4d-b0cd-f26c0113ad4a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to Static Media.
* AI translations are adding ‘[hallucinations](https://substack.com/redirect/7906fd01-6d8b-4b0a-a0e3-26c9ee395df4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to Wikipedia articles.
* [DraftKings](https://substack.com/redirect/021ed684-3c3e-4024-81a0-67b3689331a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is the king of Discord, and that’s a big deal.
	+ “We are providing guidance to developers” - Discord promises a fix and “[additional protections](https://substack.com/redirect/96f868af-7770-4e14-8534-c02a88804b87?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” following discovery of Arc Raiders bug that stored users’ private messages.

###### The vast majority of Republican midterm advertisements for House and Senate candidates are about Donald Trump, according to [Axios analysis](https://substack.com/redirect/a97cde00-bf6f-4a36-a048-3b7ffb71171d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of AdImpact data on ad airings between Jan. 1, 2025, to Feb. 18, 2026.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!_7sy!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5ca396aa-1263-4fd9-be9d-7c05be844281_1512x1111.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/ce0e80ff-bc6d-4be5-bd12-f38b282db2df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)
> With fierce competition in both the [Democratic and Republican primaries](https://substack.com/redirect/b41e5933-bb1a-4f4b-bb1c-693c9b30f3cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the 2026 Texas Senate race has already seen over $122M, making it the most expensive Senate primary on record. This year’s Texas contest became the first Senate primary to ever reach $110M, as it officially surpassed Arizona’s 2022 Senate primary, which had held the record at $109.5M.

> 👺 Report Break: Roughly 1 in 4 corporate affairs and communications leaders say they will dedicate [more than 10% of their budget to AI tools](https://substack.com/redirect/ff50c3e3-e7ed-4cf7-b19f-eac33f3f5820?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), but a majority (88%) aren't prepared to lead the AI transformation for their function.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!xYQU!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa229cf17-1fa3-4058-bf6c-5aacbef69302_1532x771.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/7d3c0fd3-c9cc-4a5f-9cdc-f89d678fa7fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)
> Corporate Affairs & Comms searching for investment sweet spot, while counterparts like Marketing commonly [spend ~25% of functional budget](https://substack.com/redirect/f9974024-20f8-44b3-9a7a-cd4773098cb2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on tech and capability building to fuel AI transformation. Half of Corporate Affairs & Comms leaders plan to redeploy or reduce headcount in the next 12 months.

> 🪫 Report Break: Again, I told ya! Half of agencies’ proprietary AI platforms will [either wind down or become obsolete](https://substack.com/redirect/027ba535-1a8c-4126-a5a7-9a8d7d09625f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by 2029, researcher Gartner predicts. That’s because agency-built offerings need to contend with the rise of open-source AI platforms. Open-source platforms are expected by Gartner to support more than 75% of enterprise AI deployments by 2028.

* How Jeffrey Epstein used [LinkedIn CEO Reid Hoffman](https://substack.com/redirect/728067f1-0f64-408b-9a3e-3d40786f7c76?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to court silicon valley’s elite.
* Microsoft tests [AI-generated Discover feed](https://substack.com/redirect/8f429ebb-dbf2-4824-9654-30caa4ce797e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Copilot web.
* Bing Search tests [Go To Shopping](https://substack.com/redirect/a202b154-4cd9-44fd-b0cb-b4dca2a37546?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) button.
* OpenAI is developing an internal alternative to [Microsoft’s GitHub](https://substack.com/redirect/ffd191f8-a410-47d8-8d11-a89fa20ab384?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Microsoft’s big developer conference [returns to San Francisco](https://substack.com/redirect/4039ccd1-05a1-42ba-9d5f-3795edd6d940?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in June.
* OpenAI had banned military use. The Pentagon [tested its models through Microsoft](https://substack.com/redirect/04c476ee-71a4-4112-adfd-471d0b3b6d7f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) anyway.
* Microsoft is reportedly [planning a new 365 tier](https://substack.com/redirect/9eace72e-bcf1-45a2-acdf-022d076aef3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) which charges AI agents like humans.

---

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!WMjD!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F605790ce-ce9b-44a1-a77e-bc12503caa70_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/aa928ca1-f4e5-498d-93f9-25b5c03589c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Wickes, the DIY chain, creates [a retail media network](https://substack.com/redirect/5d8c2eaf-3080-4399-a136-d3f1b5cff3fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* ‘[The conversation has shifted](https://substack.com/redirect/18513f4d-446a-4326-98d4-6dcb1b71d71e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: The CFO moved upstream. Now agencies have to as well.
* Onetag [acquires](https://substack.com/redirect/fbe9c1e2-1aa8-4331-8963-159eb9a47337?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Aryel, Italian Ad Tech Startup.
* E-commerce, [advertising](https://substack.com/redirect/bb4b2ef1-ae51-44a7-b702-88000d8ea41a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) will be key to Target’s turnaround.
* Trade Desk ups bid to improve CTV advertising value with [Ventura Ecosystem](https://substack.com/redirect/e5289b12-bd15-476f-8a27-2b6ab17c3936?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Smartly integrates with [Amazon DSP](https://substack.com/redirect/aeda408a-b0ee-47d3-a349-ac43fb863877?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to expand CTV capabilities globally.
* [ITV](https://substack.com/redirect/d38284d8-15fe-4ce0-ab1b-9e1c90e99238?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) remains in Sky deal talks, posts slight full-year studios profit drop, ad revenue falls 5 percent.
	+ AI is [reducing costs](https://substack.com/redirect/7be36151-8fb0-4c62-98f2-bf58a5fd2581?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at ITV as studios business offsets ad declines.
* AudienceProject [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/f52c5f9f-ac1c-458e-b6a4-c1962c7d6eb7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) cross-media measurement SaaS platform in Poland.
* [US universities](https://substack.com/redirect/da1684f2-9c04-4aad-af2a-f23eb4207051?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) lean on endowments as costs rise and funding cuts bite.
* Yahoo pauses [IAB membership](https://substack.com/redirect/91acb845-99cd-49a6-a24d-1b3d1570a0c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) amid a series of quiet cost-saving measures.
* [Little Dot Studios](https://substack.com/redirect/c5664e4e-9bff-48a7-a619-b8b7956691bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), ZDF renew content licensing [partnership](https://substack.com/redirect/6019d3e3-4cee-40d1-b9d2-608430a303fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Why brands should [stop fighting negative reviews](https://substack.com/redirect/afa90c1b-2c69-489b-9e87-76e251360d3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and start embracing them.
* PulsePoint scales [HCP omnichannel offering](https://substack.com/redirect/5a9d3e27-5990-4e4d-ab68-06905cbbc01c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Reddit integration.
* [MFE](https://substack.com/redirect/cbcd09f5-029e-4c0a-85d6-652d0eb85d31?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) sets new international top management structure.
* UK February [box office down 18%](https://substack.com/redirect/5136f171-80de-417b-ba3e-3a69bafe38b1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) amid meagre film slate.

> 🧟‍♂️ Report Break: Social media engagement is uneven — and it’s shifting. Year over year ([from 2024 → 2025](https://substack.com/redirect/525a9a2f-dd80-46aa-8118-0930288f01e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)), platforms moved in different directions:

> * Up: X (~+44%), Pinterest (~+23%), Facebook (~+11%)
> * Flat-ish: TikTok (+~3%)
> * Down: LinkedIn (~-5%), Threads (~-18%), Instagram (~-26%)
> 

---

Back to…

![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!EHMV!,w_56,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F188611d4-2dc9-4638-8d15-ac280d8870bc_500x500.png)The Social Juice 

[AI won't kill marketing. We'll do it ourselves.](https://substack.com/redirect/8c30cc07-7178-4f86-b08b-dbe48546f891?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

So far, I’ve seen dozens of marketers applauding Ben Affleck and Matthew McConaughey for their recent takes on AI, emphasizing that the technology is already here, so there’s no logic in ignoring it. Their videos are often paired with statements like this one…

[Read more](https://substack.com/redirect/8c30cc07-7178-4f86-b08b-dbe48546f891?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

a day ago · 41 likes · Jaskaran

***The Social Juice+** includes access to newsletter archive and strategy newsletters. **It’s your decision, But you are losing all three benefits**. When you can also expense it:* [Generate](https://substack.com/redirect/65f0239f-24c7-4ff1-b82f-90e4649724f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) an Employee Expense Email for Your Manager.

*\* **Others:** [Student Discount](https://substack.com/redirect/916f5555-950a-4d00-92a5-a91441c09eb7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) & Localised pricing for [Asia](https://substack.com/redirect/6c6bcf7f-255c-4fb7-8472-f2afdd7f35e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), [Eastern Europe](https://substack.com/redirect/10f178c8-6176-44d5-be17-f58223dd1964?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), [South America](https://substack.com/redirect/c6b942c4-9eaa-408d-a1dc-34ba357711a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) & [Africa](https://substack.com/redirect/a6d76eb1-7d31-4af6-af68-511d6db1c11b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).* 

*\* I use illustrations from William Heath Robinson for my heading titles, his book William Heath Robinson Inventions is a creative genius. I love it.*

<h3 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: normal;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 4px 0 0;color: #777777;line-height: 24px;font-size: 18px;margin-top: 12px;">What was that? <div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is sponsored by you… and you need to read the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1a81d8c2-a2b9-481e-9a8a-a8d779d83a80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">newsletter that came out last night</a><span>, titled: </span><strong>AI won’t kill marketing. We’ll do it ourselves.</strong></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money – you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>

<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="728"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/36d4c86a-11fa-44e8-b34c-41ff1b606bc2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":728,"bytes":50683,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!VaNH!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Supreme Court declines </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5710cb88-69ba-4f8c-a449-cdc60257dccc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI copyright case</a><span>, extending legal setback for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1fafdd10-088b-4385-9324-18db31ac9088?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-generated material</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The U.S. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dad65548-7040-4423-9991-491d6feef7ec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unexpectedly loses</a><span> 92,000 jobs in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d7bb3958-6eed-414e-9791-0def0de2512f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">February</a><span>; unemployment rate rises to 4.4%.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X reinvents </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d28c5a51-6a05-4def-95a1-dcde57074d20?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Creator Subscriptions</a><span> with new features, like </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3cd3fb0b-b070-4ca7-9500-01262d4cb8f0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">exclusive threads</a><span>, and shareable cards.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/23b0f20e-e8a9-4cee-b305-f30124660fd2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI</a><span> held early talks with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/54b2dc73-6e69-4bc1-bfb7-553d22eb7de5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Trade Desk</a><span> to sell ads.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Trade Desk CEO </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f1f5dfd8-baa1-472e-997c-4f24ee4e8894?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jeff Green</a><span> dropped $148M on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5b731527-0d55-4f1d-8c35-e63ca929a1ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">company stock</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/400ce259-cdf6-4fe5-aba0-da450e941deb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta</a><span> has a new way to measure social engagement (</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/99d03c24-8aaa-494e-9d11-3906b584c356?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">because clicks don’t cut it</a><span>).</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google to introduce </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/148eb0c3-cdb7-48c6-beaa-3518f7c2bdd4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">30-second unskippable ads</a><span> on YouTube TV </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/02e781bf-317f-4b9c-8e4a-05b5e548c7c4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">worldwide</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>

<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Your attention keeps this newsletter alive. It lets me spend time managing your trust, not trust management. As I always say…Attention is like money, you can accidentally spend it to drunk-buy clothes that don’t fit you it or you can invest it. If you invest it in this newsletter, you’ll get dividends for years…</p>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Oracle to start layoffs which </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7353462d-7fcb-447a-a044-b8d2e077be54?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">may go up to 30,000</a><span> as company faces </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/717f16fd-ef03-45f7-b8cb-a3e550b45d12?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI Cash Crunch</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AI actor Tilly Norwood’s world is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/16bfd26c-522f-444c-ba5b-ca04ec29b344?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">expanding</a><span> with the ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/be27316b-bbed-4657-bd1f-487d1770d84d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tillyverse</a><span>.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Cluely CEO Roy Lee admits to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e4c8aa3a-59ab-46ca-8c04-97c9ac61ac89?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">publicly lying about revenue numbers</a><span> last year.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9d7179d9-4be8-447b-97c2-2cb10abaf43e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">LLMs</a><span> can </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/adc5b919-6b13-45ce-81d3-69fc468b3780?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unmask pseudonymous users</a><span> at scale with surprising accuracy.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bc9e2b40-eccd-4fa2-9e56-d0755c2be21f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The secretive company</a><span> filling video game sites with gambling and AI.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Online ads just became the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/caade709-71b6-42b1-a36f-2f591488c425?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">internet’s biggest malware machine</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Apple </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a6e5f959-9db4-4129-a1e8-cefe6fe5861a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launches</a><span> first-ever budget MacBook, it’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0d562052-e3cf-4738-876b-d48768150418?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">beautiful</a><span>. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1a81d8c2-a2b9-481e-9a8a-a8d779d83a80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">I told ya!</a><span> OpenAI </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/631bba97-ce89-4f33-a62e-23bbc400690f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">scales back</a><span> shopping plans for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ded2cc5d-081a-49b3-8e0b-13391c74260c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ChatGPT</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta tests </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3fcd719e-e195-4dc4-94af-b3a1940fbf53?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI shopping research tool</a><span> to rival ChatGPT, Gemini.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cd9c45a6-187d-48c5-bcd3-9bea6cfd42f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google</a><span> faces lawsuit after Gemini chatbot allegedly instructed </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/275b4e56-d380-4685-8aa4-0801bb69fb32?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">man to kill himself</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Ad agencies are embracing ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e2331b1e-42e4-4307-b9e1-3df73d123434?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">vibe coding</a><span>’ to build GEO products for clients.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Inside an </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0fe6f5b8-554f-423f-826f-0fc49587d225?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI Slop Factory</a><span>, made for ad fraud.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">“This means each page may cost less than $2.25 to generate while creating hundreds of distinct ad serving opportunities. At that price, it doesn’t take too many impressions to recoup production costs. Everything else is pure margin.”</p></li></ul>
</li></ul>
</li>
</ul>

<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🫠 Happy International Women’s Day? Almost a third of generation Z men and boys think a wife should </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/894d0ec1-8d7f-45eb-a25d-aec369fbb35c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">obey her husband</a><span>, according to a global survey of 23,000 people that found young men hold more traditional views about gender roles than older generations.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>A third (33%) of Gen Z males also said a husband should have the final word on important decisions, according to the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/80cb86af-43a4-4451-86c9-8a707067c0d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">29-country survey</a><span>, which included Great Britain, the US, Brazil, Australia and India.</span></p>
</blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok says it </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/302fd278-f783-43a6-9ef4-b16363d76baf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">won’t encrypt DMs</a><span> claiming it puts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/de8b1290-9bfd-46ab-85cf-4c1bda7bdbea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">users at risk</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Apple users in the U.S can no longer download </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d5ed1a7a-0992-46ff-9ba9-5bde466c9f0a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ByteDance’s Chinese apps</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>“</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1f78ad09-2781-47d1-9b7c-a4d6dbcb5c14?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">What am I gonna do in a submarine?</a><span>”: Young Americans joke about a potential draft with Village People’s “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a290f0f9-3a48-4775-a075-966fb0e65979?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">In The Navy</a><span>.”</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span data-component-name="MentionStatic" style="white-space: nowrap;cursor: pointer;color: #e85c57;border-radius: 4px;transition: color .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),background-color .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),box-shadow .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),border .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),border-radius .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),opacity .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),filter .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),stroke .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),transform .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),scale .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),outline .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1);margin: 0 -.125em;padding: 0 .125em .0625em;text-decoration: none;"><a href="https://open.substack.com/users/1153079-taylor-lorenz?utm\_source=mentions" style="-webkit-text-decoration: none;color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Taylor Lorenz</a></span><span>: The TikTokers reading the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e4c7ed56-eb5e-419e-ac9a-ed05ed6fe20d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Epstein files</a><span> so you don’t have to…</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/09cfa06d-c7ec-44c8-965b-6d4b0ac9fa45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok down</a><span> for some in US, thanks to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/59e42c50-0011-47fc-857a-433472bcd980?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">second Oracle outage</a><span> since sale.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok starts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/282dfbb2-f37f-4cd7-8b41-cf0af4f1e45e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">court battle</a><span> to save China ties.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/410827ca-7d7f-4331-b2e7-f502f68b00b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">President Trump, Pam Bondi</a><span> sued over sale of TikTok assets.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Trump’s TikTok deal benefited firms that ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8d6ae0e8-b0c1-4eee-ab75-1d297466584b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">personally enriched</a><span>’ him, lawsuit says.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Seller reactions to TikTok Shop </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8de68e45-e920-49b5-b178-7ecc77742f5d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">changing stance</a><span> on shipping and fulfillment.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok partners with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4554e45b-d8e0-4989-8017-69bbcc778feb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Samba TV</a><span> on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/de429bcf-f3a5-409a-8de1-7abf8884866b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Box Office Lift measurement</a><span> and highlights that 60% of ticket purchases attributed to TikTok came from audiences not exposed to linear TV advertising.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok publishes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/711fd128-b94b-4632-8704-60d59543045a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sixth transparency report</a><span> on content moderation in Europe.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Bytedance’s open-weight Helios model brings </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bae415fe-ba5a-4081-92a6-7d5aaeea9f6e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">minute-long AI video generation</a><span> close to real time.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok owner ByteDance signs </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cce34a20-7f27-4617-9a1c-9d03b55467a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">500MW data center deal</a><span> with Vnet in China- report.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🫒 Report Break: A recent RTS study of 1,000 shoppers found that, on average, Gen Z consumers (born between 1997 and 2012) made 20 purchases on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7b47faab-e1be-41a2-86ce-9277c4a31835?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Roblox</a><span> over the last 12 months. This represents a 54% year-on-year rise, outpacing TikTok’s 10%. Gen Z placed the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/798354c6-9790-432d-b36e-4f8a80d5eeca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">highest number of Roblox orders</a><span>, making 2.4× more purchases than any other age group.</span></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
